Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the riemann_surfaces structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 432. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 847 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the riemann_surfaces space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 432 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 847. QED.
